1382885 1833615       03:03 Ss   lelinfe+ /bin/bash -lc ev='本地实验/R-W4-4/V092/partial-ranking' build="$ev/stage/build-v092" log="$ev/v092-local-command-results.log" source /usr/local/Ascend/ascend-toolkit/set_env.sh export LD_LIBRARY_PATH="/usr/lib/aarch64-linux-gnu${LD_LIBRARY_PATH:+:$LD_LIBRARY_PATH}" start_utc=$(date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S.%NZ) printf 'REVISION=V092\nGATE=INTERLEAVED_LOCAL\nPARENT=R31B-V011\nPARENT_SHA256=a8c19a1972207acc67e3fb0cd393cc70b0a4b183d1eaf5610edf80c2879b15e3\nCANDIDATE_SHA256=f4b3623b4a8cb86a43328f5dbc15fe6826ab8f13cbcc88745d0e23aa9da2b49f\nDEVICE=4\nDTYPE=FP32\nSHAPES=128x1016,128x1024,128x1032\nWARMUP=45\nSAMPLES_PER_BLOCK=31\nBLOCKS=2\nGAP_SEC=0\nBATCH_N=64\nPAIRS_PER_SHAPE=6\nORDER_PER_SHAPE=01:P,C;02:C,P;03:P,C;04:C,P;05:P,C;06:C,P\nPARTIAL_CORRECTNESS=YES\nLOCAL_SCORE_COMPARABLE_TO_OFFICIAL=NO\nC15=EXCLUDED_KNOWN_PARENT_FAILURE\nSTART_UTC=%s\n' "$start_utc" > "$log" result=PASS snapshot_pair() {   pair="$1"; width="$2"; phase="$3"   stem="$ev/v092-local-pair${pair}-r128-w${width}-fp32-${phase}"   date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S.%NZ > "${stem}-timestamp.txt"   npu-smi info > "${stem}-npu-smi-info.txt" 2>&1   printf '%s\n' "$?" > "${stem}-npu-smi-info.rc"   npu-smi info -t usages -i 4 > "${stem}-usage.txt" 2>&1   printf '%s\n' "$?" > "${stem}-usage.rc"   ps -eo pid=,ppid=,etime=,stat=,user=,args= > "${stem}-processes.txt" 2>&1   printf '%s\n' "$?" > "${stem}-ps.rc" } run_one() {   pair="$1"; width="$2"; variant="$3"   bin="$build/clx_ref_${variant}_probe"   prefix="$ev/v092-local-pair${pair}-${variant}-r128-w${width}-fp32"   start=$(date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S.%NZ)   printf 'START_UTC=%s\nCOMMAND=%s 4 128 %s 0 %s 45 31 2 0 64\n' "$start" "$bin" "$width" "$prefix" >> "$log"   "$bin" 4 128 "$width" 0 "$prefix" 45 31 2 0 64 > "${prefix}-stdout.log" 2> "${prefix}-stderr.log"   rc=$?   line=$(rg -m1 '^REF_HARNESS ' "${prefix}-stdout.log" || true)   bad=$(printf '%s\n' "$line" | sed -n 's/.* bad=\([0-9][0-9]*\).*/\1/p')   max_abs=$(awk -F '\t' '$1=="max_abs" {print $2}' "${prefix}-stats.txt" 2>/dev/null | head -1)   [ -n "$bad" ] || bad=UNKNOWN   [ -n "$max_abs" ] || max_abs=UNKNOWN   printf 'rc=%s bad=%s max_abs=%s\n' "$rc" "$bad" "$max_abs" > "${prefix}-result.txt"   end=$(date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S.%NZ)   printf 'RESULT pair=%s variant=%s shape=128x%s %s rc=%s bad=%s max_abs=%s\nEND_UTC=%s\n' "$pair" "$variant" "$width" "$line" "$rc" "$bad" "$max_abs" "$end" >> "$log"   if [ "$rc" -ne 0 ] || [ "$bad" != 0 ]; then result=FAIL; fi } for width in 1016 1024 1032; do   for i in 1 2 3 4 5 6; do     pair=$(printf '%02d' "$i")     snapshot_pair "$pair" "$width" pre     if [ $((i % 2)) -eq 1 ]; then       run_one "$pair" "$width" parent       run_one "$pair" "$width" candidate     else       run_one "$pair" "$width" candidate       run_one "$pair" "$width" parent     fi     snapshot_pair "$pair" "$width" post     printf 'PAIR_DONE shape=128x%s pair=%s status=%s\n' "$width" "$pair" "$result"   done done end_utc=$(date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S.%NZ) printf 'END_UTC=%s\nRESULT=%s\n' "$end_utc" "$result" >> "$log" printf 'LOCAL_END_UTC=%s RESULT=%s\n' "$end_utc" "$result" exit 0
